LOCAL AUTHORITIES ELECTIONS ORDINANCE (CHAPTER 262)

Notice under Section 66(2)

IT is hereby declared that the names of the members elected for each ward of the Local Authority indicated in Column IIof the Schedule below of the Local Authority Specified in Column II situated in the administrative district specified inColumn I and the names of members returned for the Local Authority are as specified below.

LOCAL AUTHORITIES ELECTIONS ORDINANCE (CHAPTER 262)

Notice under Section 66(2)

IT is hereby declared that the names of the members elected for each ward of the Local Authority indicated in Column IIof the Schedule below of the Local Authority Specified in Column II situated in the administrative district specified inColumn I and the names of members returned for the Local Authority are as specified below.

LOCAL AUTHORITIES ELECTIONS ORDINANCE (CHAPTER 262)

Notice under Section 66(2)

It is hereby declared that the names of the members elected for each ward of the Local Authority indicated in Column II of theSchedule below of the Local Authority Specified in Column II situated in the administrative district specified in Column I andthe names of members returned for the Local Authority are as specified below.
